2026-10-09T13:02:44.1530546Z   QWEN_COMMIT_MESSAGE: [cluster:aspire2ap] [nodes:2] [profile] Capture single-decode kernels at ~60 running requests (P3/D1 c72)

Why: P3/D1 with decode pool 393216 at c72 (ed8c534, 3313.0 good tok/s) is
limited by the single decode worker. Its ITL grows ~0.155 ms per running
request (~0.35 ms per verify step per request), from ~10.2 ms at 17 to
~17.2 ms at 62. MoE and dense projection GEMMs at these sizes stream the
same weights regardless of batch, so the slope is expected to come from
per-request work (target attention over ~4.1k FP8 KV with page_size 1,
draft steps, logits gather/verify) or from host gaps, not weight reads.
Decode CUTLASS was already rejected (b04c6ba). A kernel-level view at the
real batch is needed before choosing the next change.

Change (profile mode only; normal runs are byte-identical):
- When PROFILE=true on ASPIRE, only the decode worker runs under the
  externally mounted Nsight Systems 2025.3.2 (read-only, same path as the
  September decode capture f4f4aa4) with --capture-range=cudaProfilerApi,
  --cuda-graph-trace=node, no CPU sampling. Prefill workers are unchanged.
- capture_decode_profile waits for AIPerf's profiling phase, waits 20 s
  for steady state, opens a 3 s window via /start_profile
  {"activities":["CUDA_PROFILER"]} and /stop_profile on the decode worker,
  then waits for the report. The Nsight path is a script constant, not a
  new relay environment variable.
- Serving condition is exactly ed8c534 (P3/D1, decode pool 393216, Triton
  decode MoE, c72, same image 182fbb6d). The score of this instrumented
  run is not an adoption measurement.

Analysis plan: export the report to SQLite on the login node; per verify
step split target graph kernels into attention, MoE GEMM, dense GEMM,
all-reduce, logits/sampling and draft, compare GPU busy time with step
wall time to expose host gaps, and relate each to the running batch.

Budget: myprojects 59010014 balance 14714.772 SU, In Doubt 1024 (b04c6ba
not yet settled). Session spend so far ~1265 SU; this job reserves at
most 1024 SU (~420 expected).

Validation: bash -n, Ruff, full CPU pytest (new test asserts only the
decode argv is wrapped by nsys with the bounded capture range), and a
stubbed dry run of capture_decode_profile against a fake AIPerf log.
